🧭 Sim-to-Real / Simulation

· 发展脉络· Development

Sim-to-Real 领域正经历从传统域随机化向基于生成式世界模型（World Models）的范式转变，旨在解决高保真物理仿真与真实机器人执行之间的巨大鸿沟。早期工作侧重于构建高保真仿真环境（如 ManiSkill3）和采集真实数据（如 DexCap），而近期研究（2025-2026）则聚焦于利用视频生成模型和物理可编辑世界模型来合成无限多样的训练数据。最新趋势表明，通过引入触觉、力控及物理一致性约束，结合 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型，系统正逐步实现从被动观察到主动预测、从单一任务到长程复杂操作的零样本迁移。The Sim-to-Real domain is undergoing a paradigm shift from traditional domain randomization to generative world model-based approaches, aiming to bridge the vast gap between high-fidelity physical simulation and real-world robot execution. Early work focused on building high-fidelity simulation environments (e.g., ManiSkill3) and collecting real-world data (e.g., DexCap), while recent research (2025-2026) concentrates on leveraging video generation models and physically editable world models to synthesize infinitely diverse training data. Latest trends indicate that by incorporating tactile, force-control, and physical consistency constraints alongside VLA (Vision-Language-Action) models, systems are gradually achieving zero-shot transfer from passive observation to active prediction and from single tasks to long-horizon complex operations.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 生成式仿真与合成数据Generative Simulation and Synthetic Data
  • Sim-to-Real 迁移与对齐策略Sim-to-Real Transfer and Alignment Strategies
  • 低成本数据采集与遥操作Low-cost data collection and teleoperation
  • 高效仿真引擎与渲染Efficient simulation engine and rendering
  • 世界模型安全与评估World Model Safety and Evaluation
  • 强化学习信用分配与多模态对齐Reinforcement Learning Credit Assignment and Multi-modal Alignment
  • 世界模型与物理因果推断World Models and Physical Causal Inference

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

生成式仿真与合成数据Generative Simulation and Synthetic Data

生成式仿真与合成数据旨在利用扩散模型、世界模型及 AIGC 技术解决具身智能数据稀缺与物理交互高保真难题。早期工作如 Genie 尝试构建可交互视频世界，但受限于物理一致性与长程幻觉；随后 PhyScene、RoboCasa 等引入物理约束与大规模资产生成，聚焦场景与任务的可操作性；近期研究如 DreamGen、GraspVLA 及 RoboTwin 系列，通过视频世界模型反推动作、构建亿级合成数据集，显著提升了 Sim-to-Real 迁移能力。演进趋势从单纯视觉生成转向物理可解、接触感知及多模态协同，正逐步构建低成本、高泛化的通用机器人训练场。Generative simulation and synthetic data aim to utilize diffusion models, world models, and AIGC technologies to address the scarcity of embodied intelligence data and the challenge of high-fidelity physical interaction. Early work like Genie attempted to build interactive video worlds but was limited by physical consistency and long-horizon hallucinations; subsequently, PhyScene, RoboCasa, and others introduced physical constraints and large-scale asset generation, focusing on scene and task operability; recent research such as DreamGen, GraspVLA, and the RoboTwin series reverse-engineer actions via video world models and construct billion-scale synthetic datasets, significantly enhancing Sim-to-Real transfer capabilities. The evolutionary trend shifts from pure visual generation to physically solvable, contact-aware, and multi-modal collaboration, gradually building low-cost, high-generalization general-purpose robot training grounds.

2024-02
Genie: Generative Interactive Environments
动机: Motivation: 解决视频生成模型缺乏交互性与物理因果推理的问题，探索仅凭视频数据自学操作策略的可能性。Address the lack of interactivity and physical causal reasoning in video generation models, exploring the possibility of self-learning manipulation strategies solely from video data.
方法: Method: 基于 11B 参数 MaskGIT 架构构建视频生成世界模型，通过自回归预测未来帧实现无标签交互生成。Construct a video generation world model based on the 11B parameter MaskGIT architecture, achieving label-free interactive generation through autoregressive prediction of future frames.
推进: Advance: 首次证明纯视频数据可隐式学习操作逻辑，但受限于低帧率与长程一致性幻觉，物理交互尚不成熟。Firstly proved that pure video data can implicitly learn manipulation logic, but is limited by low frame rates and long-horizon consistency hallucinations, with physical interaction still immature.
2024-06
RoboCasa: Large-Scale Simulation of Everyday Tasks for Generalist Robots
动机: Motivation: 应对通用机器人训练数据稀缺问题，利用生成式 AI 低成本构建大规模厨房场景与复合任务轨迹。Address the scarcity of general-purpose robot training data by using generative AI to low-cost construct large-scale kitchen scenes and composite task trajectories.
方法: Method: 结合 LLM 生成复合任务指令与 AI 生成资产，在 RoboSuite 物理引擎中构建包含 10 万条轨迹的 RoboCasa 数据集。Combine LLM-generated composite task instructions with AI-generated assets to build the RoboCasa dataset containing 100,000 trajectories within the RoboSuite physics engine.
推进: Advance: 验证了合成数据在原子技能上的缩放有效性，但复合任务在真实世界的迁移成功率仍较低，暴露了物理鸿沟。Verified the scaling effectiveness of synthetic data on atomic skills, but the transfer success rate of composite tasks in the real world remains low, exposing the physical gap.
2024-10
OS-ATLAS: A Foundation Action Model for Generalist GUI Agents
动机: Motivation: 克服桌面端 GUI 操作数据稀缺，构建跨平台、大规模的合成指令与屏幕交互数据集以训练通用 GUI 智能体。Overcome the scarcity of desktop GUI operation data by constructing cross-platform, large-scale synthetic instruction and screen interaction datasets to train general-purpose GUI agents.
方法: Method: 开发跨平台数据合成工具，利用 GPT-4o 生成指令数据并合成 1300 万个屏幕元素，构建 OS-ATLAS 基准。Develop cross-platform data synthesis tools, utilizing GPT-4o to generate instruction data and synthesize 13 million screen elements to build the OS-ATLAS benchmark.
推进: Advance: 大幅提升了 GUI 智能体在 ScreenSpot 等基准的表现，但依赖大模型生成指令可能导致错误传播，且难以完全替代物理机采集。Significantly improved the performance of GUI agents on benchmarks like ScreenSpot, but reliance on large models for instruction generation may lead to error propagation and cannot fully replace physical machine data collection.
2024-10
DexGraspNet 2.0: Learning Generative Dexterous Grasping in Large-scale Synthetic Cluttered Scenes
动机: Motivation: 解决灵巧手在杂乱场景下抓取数据稀缺问题，利用生成式方法构建大规模多模态抓取数据集。Address the scarcity of grasping data for dexterous hands in cluttered scenes by utilizing generative methods to construct large-scale multi-modal grasping datasets.
方法: Method: 基于扩散模型生成 4.27 亿个抓取标签，结合局部特征条件与深度恢复技术，优化 Sim-to-Real 迁移。Generate 427 million grasping labels based on diffusion models, combining local feature conditions and depth recovery technologies to optimize Sim-to-Real transfer.
推进: Advance: 在静态杂乱场景下实现了 90.7% 的实机成功率，证明了生成式数据在提升抓取泛化性上的巨大潜力，但动态交互能力仍受限。Achieved a 90.7% real-robot success rate in static cluttered scenes, proving the huge potential of generative data in improving grasping generalization, but dynamic interaction capabilities remain limited.
2024-10
DexMimicGen: Automated Data Generation for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation via Imitation Learning
动机: Motivation: 针对双灵巧手操作数据获取困难，探索利用少量真人演示在仿真中克隆海量高质量数据的工程化方案。Address the difficulty in acquiring dual-dexterous-hand operation data by exploring engineering solutions to clone massive high-quality data in simulation using a small number of human demonstrations.
方法: Method: 基于 MimicGen 框架扩展，利用 SE(3) 变换假设与仿真物理引擎，将 60 个真人 Demo 扩展为 21K 合成数据。Based on the MimicGen framework extension, utilizing SE(3) transformation assumptions and simulation physics engines to expand 60 human demos into 21K synthetic data.
推进: Advance: 实现了 90% 的真机部署成功率，证明了仿真数据增强在特定任务上的有效性，但对复杂接触与非刚体场景的泛化性存疑。Achieved a 90% real-robot deployment success rate, proving the effectiveness of simulation data augmentation on specific tasks, but generalization to complex contact and non-rigid scenarios is questionable.
2024-12
DriveEditor: A Unified 3D Information-Guided Framework for Controllable Object Editing in Driving Scenes
动机: Motivation: 为自动驾驶感知模型提供低成本、高可控的数据增强方案，解决真实场景标注昂贵且稀缺的问题。Provide a low-cost, highly controllable data augmentation solution for autonomous driving perception models, addressing the problem of expensive and scarce real-world scene annotation.
方法: Method: 构建统一 3D 信息引导框架，结合视频编辑与 3D 感知，实现可控制的目标编辑与场景合成。Construct a unified 3D information-guided framework, combining video editing and 3D perception to achieve controllable target editing and scene synthesis.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了下游感知模型的 mRecall，但依赖完美的 3D 边界框标注，且对复杂动态场景的生成能力有限。Significantly improved the mRecall of downstream perception models, but relies on perfect 3D bounding box annotations and has limited generation capabilities for complex dynamic scenes.
2024-12
Generative Video Propagation
动机: Motivation: 旨在利用生成式视频模型统一视频编辑任务，解决传统仿真中物理交互数据稀缺且难以通过光流或深度图显式建模的难题。Aiming to unify video editing tasks using generative video models to address the scarcity of physical interaction data in traditional simulations and the difficulty of explicitly modeling it via optical flow or depth maps.
方法: Method: 提出GenProp，基于图像到视频（I2V）扩散模型，在不依赖显式光流或深度输入的情况下，通过首帧编辑直接生成具有物理合理性的后续视频帧。Proposes GenProp, based on an image-to-video (I2V) diffusion model, to directly generate subsequent video frames with physical plausibility from first-frame editing without relying on explicit optical flow or depth inputs.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需多模态输入的统一视频生成，为仿真数据提供了低成本、高自由度的视觉合成方案，尽管物理一致性仍依赖模型隐式学习。Achieves unified video generation without multi-modal inputs, providing a low-cost, high-freedom visual synthesis solution for simulation data, although physical consistency still relies on implicit model learning.
2025-01
DiffusionRenderer: Neural Inverse and Forward Rendering with Video Diffusion Models
动机: Motivation: 突破传统物理路径追踪渲染效率低下的瓶颈，利用生成式先验实现快速、高保真的神经渲染。Break through the bottleneck of low efficiency in traditional physical path tracing rendering by utilizing generative priors to achieve fast, high-fidelity neural rendering.
方法: Method: 将视频扩散模型嵌入渲染管线，通过神经逆/前向渲染技术，从单视频推断光照、材质与 G-buffer。Embed video diffusion models into the rendering pipeline, using neural inverse/forward rendering technologies to infer lighting, materials, and G-buffer from a single video.
推进: Advance: 实现了单视频输入下的高效可编辑渲染，对噪声容忍度高，但物理一致性依赖数据拟合，缺乏显式物理引擎的严谨性。Achieved efficient editable rendering under single video input with high noise tolerance, but physical consistency relies on data fitting, lacking the rigor of explicit physics engines.
2025-04
RoboTwin: Dual-Arm Robot Benchmark with Generative Digital Twins
动机: Motivation: 解决双臂机器人数据稀缺问题，利用生成式数字孪生技术降低遥操作成本并提升数据多样性。Address the scarcity of bimanual robot data by utilizing generative digital twin technology to reduce teleoperation costs and enhance data diversity.
方法: Method: 结合 3D 生成模型与 LLM，将 2D 图像转换为 3D 模型并自动生成仿真代码，构建双臂操作基准。Combine 3D generation models with LLMs to convert 2D images into 3D models and automatically generate simulation code, building a bimanual operation benchmark.
推进: Advance: 验证了生成式数据在双臂任务中的有效性，但双臂协同的算法瓶颈未解决，单纯增加数据量无法完全弥补策略缺陷。Verified the effectiveness of generative data in bimanual tasks, but the algorithmic bottleneck of bimanual coordination remains unsolved; simply increasing data volume cannot fully compensate for policy deficiencies.
2025-05
DreamGen: Unlocking Generalization in Robot Learning through Video World Models
动机: Motivation: 利用视频世界模型生成合成数据，解决机器人策略训练数据稀缺且物理交互难以模拟的问题。Utilize video world models to generate synthetic data, addressing the scarcity of robot policy training data and the difficulty of simulating physical interactions.
方法: Method: 微调视频世界模型生成视频，利用逆动力学模型（IDM）反推动作，构建 DreamGen 基准与训练策略。Fine-tune video world models to generate videos, utilize inverse dynamics models (IDM) to reverse-engineer actions, and construct the DreamGen benchmark and training strategies.
推进: Advance: 实现了在少量真机数据下学习 22 种新任务并泛化到新环境，证明了视频世界模型作为数据生成器的潜力，但计算成本高昂。Achieved learning 22 new tasks and generalizing to new environments with a small amount of real-robot data, proving the potential of video world models as data generators, but with high computational costs.
2025-06
RoboTwin 2.0: A Scalable Data Generator and Benchmark with Strong Domain Randomization for Robust Bimanual Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 扩展双臂操作数据集规模，通过生成式数据与强域随机化提升策略在复杂任务下的泛化与鲁棒性。Expanded the scale of dual-arm manipulation datasets, enhancing strategy generalization and robustness in complex tasks through generative data and strong domain randomization.
方法: Method: 利用 MLLM 生成代码与仿真回环，构建包含 10 万条轨迹、50 个任务的 RoboTwin 2.0 基准。Leveraged MLLMs to generate code and simulation loops, constructing the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark containing 100,000 trajectories and 50 tasks.
推进: Advance: 验证了“假”数据在特定设置下可超越纯真数据，但 Hard 设置下泛化性仍不足，暴露了合成数据质量与策略鲁棒性的矛盾。Validated that 'fake' data can surpass purely real data under specific settings, but generalization remains insufficient under Hard settings, exposing the contradiction between synthetic data quality and strategy robustness.
2025-09
PhysCtrl: Generative Physics for Controllable and Physics-Grounded Video Generation
动机: Motivation: 解决视频生成中物理规律不合理的问题，探索生成式物理网络在视频合成中的应用。Addressed the issue of unreasonable physical laws in video generation, exploring the application of generative physical networks in video synthesis.
方法: Method: 训练生成式物理网络学习物理分布，生成点轨迹并驱动视频模型，实现物理可解释的视频生成。Trained generative physical networks to learn physical distributions, generating point trajectories to drive video models, achieving physically interpretable video generation.
推进: Advance: 实现了可控的物理视频生成，但本质仍为“伪物理”，在复杂交互与薄结构处理上存在幻觉与失效风险。Achieved controllable physical video generation, but it remains essentially 'pseudo-physics', with risks of hallucinations and failures in complex interactions and thin structure handling.
2026-06
SENSE-VAD: Sentient and Semantic Video Anomaly Detection for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 解决自动驾驶感知模型在社会性交互场景（如异常行为）下的盲区，利用仿真生成高难度异常数据。Addressed the blind spots of autonomous driving perception models in social interaction scenarios (e.g., anomalous behaviors), utilizing simulation to generate high-difficulty anomalous data.
方法: Method: 基于 CARLA 仿真器构建 SENSE-VAD 数据集，专注于运动正常但社会关系异常的长尾场景。Constructed the SENSE-VAD dataset based on the CARLA simulator, focusing on long-tail scenarios where motion is normal but social relationships are anomalous.
推进: Advance: 揭示了现有 SOTA 模型在理解社会关系推理上的不足，证明了合成数据在挖掘长尾异常场景中的关键价值。Revealed the deficiencies of existing SOTA models in understanding social relationship reasoning, proving the critical value of synthetic data in mining long-tail anomalous scenarios.
2026-06
PhysEditWorld: A Large-Scale Dataset Toward Physics-Editable World Models
动机: Motivation: 解决现有视频世界模型物理规律隐式学习、不可控的问题，构建显式物理规则可编辑的数据集。Addressed the issues of implicit learning and lack of controllability of physical laws in existing video world models, constructing a dataset with explicitly editable physical rules.
方法: Method: 基于 UE5 构建 PhysEditWorld 数据集，包含 6000 万帧多模态数据，支持重力等物理参数的显式编辑。Built the PhysEditWorld dataset based on UE5, containing 60 million frames of multi-modal data, supporting explicit editing of physical parameters such as gravity.
推进: Advance: 实现了通过 LoRA 微调让模型理解并响应物理指令，为构建物理可解释的世界模型提供了关键数据基础。Achieved model understanding and response to physical instructions via LoRA fine-tuning, providing a critical data foundation for building physically interpretable world models.
2026-06
Pose6DAug: Physically Plausible Multi-view Object Swapping for Robot Data Augmentation
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型在长尾物体交互数据稀缺问题，利用物理合理的多视角物体替换进行数据增强。Addressed the scarcity of long-tail object interaction data in VLA models, utilizing physically multi-view object replacement for data augmentation.
方法: Method: 基于 3D 重建与姿态估计，将成功抓取轨迹迁移至新物体，生成物理合理的多视角合成数据。Based on 3D reconstruction and pose estimation, transferred successful grasp trajectories to new objects, generating physically consistent multi-view synthetic data.
推进: Advance: 在 VLA 模型上实现了 16.5% 的性能提升，证明了多视角一致性增强对泛化性的贡献，但依赖源数据质量与重建精度。Achieved a 16.5% performance improvement on VLA models, proving the contribution of multi-view consistency enhancement to generalization, but relying on source data quality and reconstruction accuracy.
2026-06
Do as I Do: Dexterous Manipulation Data from Everyday Human Videos
动机: Motivation: 挖掘互联网海量人类视频中的操作数据，填补“看”与“做”的鸿沟，解决灵巧操作数据稀缺问题。Exploited massive human operation data from internet videos to bridge the gap between 'seeing' and 'doing', addressing the scarcity of dexterous manipulation data.
方法: Method: 利用单目 RGB 视频进行 4D 重建与动力学重定向，从日常视频中提取机器人可执行的灵巧操作数据。Utilized monocular RGB videos for 4D reconstruction and kinematic retargeting to extract dexterous operation data executable by robots from daily videos.
推进: Advance: 实现了从非结构化视频到结构化操作数据的转化，但数据清洗成本极高，且对软体与复杂场景的泛化能力有限。Achieved the transformation from unstructured videos to structured operation data, but with extremely high data cleaning costs and limited generalization capabilities for soft bodies and complex scenarios.
2026-06
VLK: Learning Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Synthetic Interactions in Reconstructed Scenes
动机: Motivation: 解决人形机器人 locomotion-manipulation 数据稀缺问题，利用合成数据实现零样本迁移。Addressed the scarcity of locomotion-manipulation data for humanoid robots, utilizing synthetic data to achieve zero-shot transfer.
方法: Method: 结合 3DGS 重建真实场景与人类动作重定向技术，在虚拟环境中生成 48k 条合成轨迹并迁移至真机。Combined 3DGS reconstruction of real scenes with human motion retargeting techniques to generate 48k synthetic trajectories in virtual environments and transfer them to real robots.
推进: Advance: 成功实现了从合成数据到 Unitree G1 真机的零样本迁移，证明了合成数据在复杂全身操作中的工程价值。Successfully achieved zero-shot transfer from synthetic data to the Unitree G1 real robot, proving the engineering value of synthetic data in complex whole-body manipulation.
2026-07
PWM-ArtGen: Part World Model for Articulated Object Generation
动机: Motivation: 解决单图生成可动物体时结构预测与运动学参数不匹配的问题，利用世界模型联合建模视觉与运动。Addressed the mismatch between structure prediction and kinematic parameters when generating animatable objects from a single image, utilizing world models for joint modeling of vision and motion.
方法: Method: 构建 Part World Model，利用独立扩散步长协同训练视觉动态与运动学参数，生成可动部件数据。Constructed the Part World Model, utilizing independent diffusion steps to collaboratively train visual dynamics and kinematic parameters, generating animable part data.
推进: Advance: 缓解了合成到真实的鸿沟，实现了零样本泛化，但依赖黑盒分割模型且对非标准结构的物理先验仍显不足。Mitigated the sim-to-real gap and achieved zero-shot generalization, but relies on black-box segmentation models and still lacks sufficient physical priors for non-standard structures.
2026-07
SimWorlds: A Multi-Agent System for Dynamic 3D Scene Creation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有生成式3D场景缺乏物理一致性与可编辑性的问题，解决仅追求视觉逼真而忽略物理求解机制的“幻觉”缺陷。Addressed the lack of physical consistency and editability in existing generative 3D scenes, solving the 'hallucination' defect of pursuing visual realism while ignoring physical solving mechanisms.
方法: Method: 构建多智能体系统，利用LLM进行逻辑判断并直接生成包含物理求解器参数的可编辑Blender文件，而非仅输出渲染图像。Constructed a multi-agent system, utilizing LLMs for logical judgment to directly generate editable Blender files containing physical solver parameters, rather than just outputting rendered images.
推进: Advance: 证明了在生成式AI时代，代码与物理机制的正确性优于像素级逼真度，为具身智能提供了稀缺的、具备物理交互能力的合成资产。Proves that in the era of generative AI, the correctness of code and physical mechanisms outweighs pixel-level photorealism, providing embodied intelligence with scarce synthetic assets possessing physical interaction capabilities.
2026-07
RoboWorld: Fast and Reliable Neural Simulators for Generalist Robot Policy Evaluation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决实地机器人评估成本高昂且耗时长的问题，试图利用视频世界模型替代昂贵的真机测试环节。Aims to address the high cost and time-consuming nature of real-world robot evaluation by attempting to utilize video world models to replace expensive real-robot testing phases.
方法: Method: 提出RoboWorld神经仿真器，采用Step Forcing机制抑制长视界生成的累积误差，并结合VLM进行自动化策略打分。Proposes the RoboWorld neural simulator, employing a Step Forcing mechanism to suppress cumulative errors in long-horizon generation, and combines VLMs for automated strategy scoring.
推进: Advance: 实现了15 FPS下的高保真长视界生成与评估，Pearson相关系数达0.989，为Sim-to-Real迁移提供了高效且可靠的低成本替代方案。Achieves high-fidelity long-horizon generation and evaluation at 15 FPS, with a Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.989, offering an efficient, reliable, and low-cost alternative for Sim-to-Real transfer.
2026-07
Unleashing More Actions via Action Compositional Training for VLA Models
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA模型在未见物体组合下泛化能力差（OOD性能低）的痛点，试图通过合成数据解决训练数据分布单一的问题。Addresses the pain point of poor generalization (low OOD performance) of VLA models under unseen object combinations, attempting to solve the problem of single-distribution training data through synthetic data.

Sim-to-Real 迁移与对齐策略Sim-to-Real Transfer and Alignment Strategies

Sim-to-Real 迁移的核心在于消除仿真与真实物理环境间的域差异。早期工作依赖域随机化（DR）和系统辨识（SysID）来增加鲁棒性，但往往牺牲性能。演进趋势转向构建高保真数字孪生（如 FOTS, TaCauchy）以精确模拟接触与触觉，或利用残差学习（ASAP）与逆向蒸馏（RialTo）补偿模型失配。近期研究进一步探索功能对齐（ACDC）而非几何完美，以及通过共享潜空间（TactSpace）解耦物理与信号差异。总体思路从“硬抗随机性”转向“精准建模”与“智能对齐”，旨在降低真机调试成本并提升策略泛化能力。The core of Sim-to-Real transfer lies in eliminating domain differences between simulation and real physical environments. Early work relied on domain randomization (DR) and system identification (SysID) to increase robustness, often at the cost of performance. The evolutionary trend shifts towards building high-fidelity digital twins (e.g., FOTS, TaCauchy) to precisely simulate contact and touch, or utilizing residual learning (ASAP) and inverse distillation (RialTo) to compensate for model mismatch. Recent research further explores functional alignment (ACDC) rather than geometric perfection, and decoupling physical and signal differences through shared latent spaces (TactSpace). The overall approach shifts from "hardly resisting randomness" to "precise modeling" and "intelligent alignment," aiming to reduce real-robot debugging costs and enhance strategy generalization.

动机: Motivation: 解决移动操作中仿真与真实世界因相机运动耦合导致的因果链断裂及感知域差异问题。This addresses the issue of causal chain breakage and perception domain differences caused by camera motion coupling between simulation and the real world in mobile manipulation tasks.
方法: Method: 提出Chain-of-Foresight机制，在训练阶段利用视频生成模型进行前瞻推理，而在推理阶段仅保留当前帧编码以适配移动端。A Chain-of-Foresight mechanism is proposed, utilizing video generation models for forward-looking inference during the training phase, while retaining only current frame encoding during the inference phase to adapt to mobile devices.
推进: Advance: 揭示了仅靠RGB输入在真实物理环境中难以克服定位误差的局限，证明了仿真高成功率无法直接迁移至真实长程任务。This reveals the limitation that RGB input alone struggles to overcome localization errors in real physical environments, proving that high success rates in simulation cannot be directly transferred to real long-horizon tasks.

方法: Method: 提出PAVE框架，将世界预测与价值评估解耦，训练阶段进行全量预测与打分进化，推理阶段仅保留精简的正反馈执行器。Proposes the PAVE framework, decoupling world prediction and value assessment, performing full prediction and scoring evolution during training, and retaining only a streamlined positive feedback executor during inference.
推进: Advance: 实现了训练时的高保真模拟与上线时的极简执行之间的平衡，证明了离线进化出的策略能显著提升真实环境下的样本效率与鲁棒性。Achieves a balance between high-fidelity simulation during training and minimal execution during deployment, proving that strategies evolved offline can significantly improve sample efficiency and robustness in real environments.

Do Robotic World Models Really Follow Actions? Diagnosing and Aligning Action-Conditioned Generation for Policy Learning
动机: Motivation: 诊断行动条件世界模型（AC-WM）在Sim-to-Real迁移中存在的“视觉逼真但动作错误”的幻觉问题，揭示专家数据无法覆盖真实物理交互的边界。Diagnoses the 'visually realistic but action-incorrect' hallucination problem in Action-Conditioned World Models (AC-WM) during Sim-to-Real transfer, revealing that expert data cannot cover the boundaries of real physical interactions.
方法: Method: 构建WorldEcho评测体系，通过SE(3)轨迹对齐与覆盖扩展数据，结合WorldSync三件套（覆盖扩展、AFE、IE）强制模型学习真实动力学。Constructs the WorldEcho evaluation system, using SE(3) trajectory alignment and coverage expansion data, combined with the WorldSync trio (coverage expansion, AFE, IE) to force models to learn real dynamics.
推进: Advance: 量化了仿真数据与真实物理交互的分布差异，证明了混合真实交互数据训练能显著缓解世界模型的“盲目自信”，提升策略在开放动态环境中的泛化。Quantifies the distributional differences between simulation data and real physical interactions, proving that training with mixed real interaction data significantly alleviates the 'blind confidence' of world models and improves strategy generalization in open dynamic environments.

动机: Motivation: 证伪世界模型能自动习得并维持物理不变量（如能量守恒）的假设，指出纯数据驱动模型在长时程Sim-to-Real rollout中因误差累积而失效。Disproves the hypothesis that world models can automatically learn and maintain physical invariants (such as energy conservation), pointing out that pure data-driven models fail in long-term Sim-to-Real rollouts due to error accumulation.
方法: Method: 通过投影修正技术显式约束世界模型中的物理不变量，并设计阻尼系统对照组验证模型在“做梦”过程中的物理一致性。Explicitly constrains physical invariants in world models through projection correction techniques and designs a damping system control group to verify the physical consistency of the model during the 'dreaming' process.
推进: Advance: 揭示了神经网络仅能拟合相关性而非因果性，证明了显式物理约束是提升Sim-to-Real长时程预测精度的必要条件，而非单纯依赖数据规模。Reveals that neural networks can only fit correlations rather than causality, proving that explicit physical constraints are a necessary condition for improving long-term prediction accuracy in Sim-to-Real, rather than relying solely on data scale.

动机: Motivation: 揭示现有模仿学习基准测试忽视时间维度鲁棒性，导致策略在仿真中表现完美但在真实加速场景下迅速失效的“过拟合”现象。Reveals that existing imitation learning benchmarks ignore temporal robustness, leading to an 'overfitting' phenomenon where strategies perform perfectly in simulation but fail rapidly in real accelerated scenarios.

推进: Advance: 证明了高名义成功率并不等同于高物理泛化能力，指出Sim-to-Real迁移必须考虑执行速度等动态约束，否则策略在真实世界极易崩溃。Proves that high nominal success rates do not equate to high physical generalization capabilities, pointing out that Sim-to-Real transfer must consider dynamic constraints such as execution speed, otherwise strategies are prone to collapse in the real world.

方法: Method: 设计LCAP方法，采用“先公后私”策略，先通过群体校准消除共性误差，再利用低维潜空间推断个体残差。Designing the LCAP method, which adopts a "public first, private later" strategy, first eliminating common errors through population calibration and then inferring individual residuals using a low-dimensional latent space.

方法: Method: 提出EIDA方法，将执行接口视为黑盒，利用真实导航数据拟合模拟器的几何与反馈机制，实现从真到仿再到真的动态域对齐。Proposing the EIDA method, treating the execution interface as a black box, using real navigation data to fit the simulator's geometry and feedback mechanisms, achieving dynamic domain alignment from real to sim and back to real.
推进: Advance: 展示了无需重建底层物理引擎即可通过数据驱动的方式显著提升Go2真机导航性能，为Sim-to-Real迁移提供了一种不依赖复杂物理建模的实用化捷径。Demonstrating that real-robot navigation performance for Go2 can be significantly improved through a data-driven approach without rebuilding the underlying physics engine, providing a practical shortcut for Sim-to-Real transfer that does not rely on complex physical modeling.

SimForcing: Distilling Simulation Motion Priors into Real-Domain Robot World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对Sim-to-Real迁移中直接对齐像素外观导致的视觉鸿沟问题，旨在利用仿真数据构建高保真的运动先验以指导真实世界模型。Aiming to address the visual gap caused by direct pixel appearance alignment in Sim-to-Real transfer, this work seeks to construct high-fidelity motion priors from simulation data to guide real-world models.
方法: Method: 提出SimForcing框架，将仿真动作先验蒸馏至真实域的世界模型，通过在潜空间计算运动差异而非外观差异来解耦物理与信号噪声。Proposes the SimForcing framework, distilling simulation action priors into real-domain world models, decoupling physics from signal noise by computing motion differences rather than appearance differences in latent space.
推进: Advance: 证明了无需预训练即可在Bridge数据集上取得SOTA，并揭示了世界模型不仅能感知还能通过仿真先验辅助策略学习，实现了从“视觉对齐”到“运动先验对齐”的范式转变。Demonstrated achieving SOTA on the Bridge dataset without pre-training, revealing that world models not only perceive but also assist policy learning via simulation priors, achieving a paradigm shift from 'visual alignment' to 'motion prior alignment'.

Dual Variational Autoencoders for Efficient Sim-to-Real Transfer in Low-Cost Robotic Navigation
动机: Motivation: 面向低成本机器人导航场景，解决在资源受限下利用混合迁移学习填补仿真与现实域差异的难题。Targeting low-cost robot navigation scenarios, this work addresses the challenge of bridging the gap between simulation and real domains using hybrid transfer learning under resource constraints.
方法: Method: 构建双变分自编码器（Dual VAE）框架，通过冻结解码器并微调编码器，利用潜在空间扰动生成伪数据以增强模型鲁棒性。Constructs a Dual VAE framework, generating pseudo-data via latent space perturbations by freezing the decoder and fine-tuning the encoder to enhance model robustness.
推进: Advance: 在树莓派等嵌入式设备上实现了91%的导航成功率，验证了低计算成本下通过潜空间特征对齐替代高保真物理建模的工程可行性。Achieved a 91% navigation success rate on embedded devices like Raspberry Pi, validating the engineering feasibility of replacing high-fidelity physical modeling with latent space feature alignment under low computational costs.

动机: Motivation: 针对传统触觉端到端迁移中几何、力与信号耦合导致域差异难以解耦的问题，旨在通过因子化策略降低仿真到真实的对齐难度。Aiming to reduce the difficulty of sim-to-real alignment through factorization strategies, addressing the challenge in traditional tactile end-to-end transfer where geometry, force, and signal coupling make domain differences hard to decouple.
方法: Method: 提出将触觉响应解耦为几何、力和时序三个独立因子，并设计Tactile Gated Policy在推理时动态选择输入因子。Proposes decoupling tactile responses into three independent factors: geometry, force, and temporal dynamics, and designs a Tactile Gated Policy to dynamically select input factors during inference.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需重新训练即可适应不同任务需求的动态迁移，证明了分而治之的因子化表征比黑盒融合更能有效缓解触觉域偏移。Achieves dynamic transfer adaptable to different task requirements without retraining, proving that factorized representation via divide-and-conquer is more effective than black-box fusion in mitigating tactile domain shift.

General-Purpose Sim2Real Protocol for Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation With Marker-Based Visuotactile Sensors
动机: Motivation: 针对接触丰富操作任务中，刚性体仿真无法准确模拟弹性体变形，导致Sim-to-Real迁移失败的老大难问题。Addressing the longstanding problem of Sim-to-Real transfer failure in contact-rich manipulation tasks, where rigid body simulation cannot accurately simulate elastic deformation.
方法: Method: 提出基于Marker-based传感器的通用协议，利用FEM（有限元方法）和IPC（隐式接触）模拟弹性体变形，结合自监督预训练。Proposing a marker-based sensor universal protocol, utilizing FEM (Finite Element Method) and IPC (Implicit Contact) to simulate elastic deformation, combined with self-supervised pre-training.
推进: Advance: 验证了回归物理本质（FEM）比堆砌Transformer更能解决接触仿真难题，但指出计算开销限制了实时性，且依赖特定传感器限制了通用性。Validating that regressing physical essence (FEM) is more effective than stacking Transformers for solving contact simulation challenges, but noting that computational overhead limits real-time performance and reliance on specific sensors limits generalizability.

低成本数据采集与遥操作Low-cost data collection and teleoperation

该方向核心在于打破高成本动捕与专用机器人硬件的壁垒，通过手持设备、单目视觉及免动捕方案实现低成本、大规模的人类演示数据采集。演进逻辑从早期的硬件简化（如手持夹爪、便携动捕）转向算法层面的域适应与数据增强（如仿真校准、视频重定向），旨在解决Sim-to-Real中的观察空间对齐与物理接触建模难题。最新趋势利用世界模型与数字孪生技术，将遥操作从物理实体解放至纯数字环境，进一步降低人力与硬件门槛，推动从单一任务模仿向长视界、高泛化策略的跨越。The core of this direction lies in breaking the barriers of high-cost motion capture and specialized robot hardware, achieving low-cost, large-scale human demonstration data collection through handheld devices, monocular vision, and motion-capture-free solutions. The evolutionary logic has shifted from early hardware simplification (e.g., handheld grippers, portable motion capture) to algorithm-level domain adaptation and data augmentation (e.g., simulation calibration, video retargeting), aiming to solve observation space alignment and physical contact modeling challenges in Sim-to-Real. The latest trend utilizes world models and digital twin technologies to liberate teleoperation from physical entities to purely digital environments, further lowering human and hardware thresholds and promoting the leap from single-task imitation to long-horizon, high-generalization strategies.

Towards Human-level Dexterous Teleoperation
动机: Motivation: 为突破传统遥操作仅能实现视觉观测而无法精确控制物理接触的局限，致力于将人类意图转化为底层接触执行策略。To break through the limitations of traditional teleoperation which only achieves visual observation but cannot precisely control physical contact, it is dedicated to translating human intent into low-level contact execution strategies.
方法: Method: 构建手物共跟踪系统TELEDEXTER，通过低层接触执行机制将人类演示意图直接转化为机器人的物理操作动作。It constructs the hand-object co-tracking system TELEDEXTER, which directly translates human demonstration intent into the robot's physical manipulation actions through a low-level contact execution mechanism.
推进: Advance: 在长程工具使用任务中实现了75%的成功率，展示了从“看得到”到“摸得着”的跨越，为通用灵巧操作提供了新的遥操作范式。It achieves a 75% success rate in long-horizon tool-use tasks, demonstrating the leap from "seeing" to "touching," and provides a new teleoperation paradigm for general dexterous manipulation.

Towards Miniature Humanoid Tele-Loco-Manipulation Using Virtual Reality and Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 旨在打破高成本专用硬件壁垒，探索利用廉价VR设备与迷你人形机器人实现低成本遥操作与Sim-to-Real迁移的可行性。Aims to break down the barriers of high-cost dedicated hardware, exploring the feasibility of achieving low-cost teleoperation and Sim-to-Real transfer using affordable VR devices and mini humanoid robots.
方法: Method: 提出VR上肢遥操作与RL下肢步态解耦的架构，结合PD控制与域随机化策略，在纯数字仿真环境中训练并迁移至物理实体。Proposes an architecture decoupling VR upper-limb teleoperation from RL lower-limb gait control, combining PD control and domain randomization strategies to train in a purely digital simulation environment and transfer to physical entities.
推进: Advance: 验证了低成本硬件平台在Sim-to-Real任务中的基本可行性，但暴露了缺乏力反馈与高域随机化依赖导致的操作效率低下问题。Validates the basic feasibility of low-cost hardware platforms in Sim-to-Real tasks, but exposes issues of low operational efficiency caused by the lack of force feedback and high dependence on domain randomization.

ReferTrack: Referring Then Tracking for Embodied Visual Tracking
动机: Motivation: 针对传统具身视觉跟踪中识别与规划混同导致的黑盒对齐难、数据标注成本高的问题，寻求低算力下的显式观测对齐方案。Addresses the difficulties in black-box alignment and high data annotation costs in traditional embodied visual tracking caused by the conflation of recognition and planning, seeking an explicit observation alignment scheme under low computing power.
方法: Method: 设计“先点名后跟踪”机制，将目标识别转化为从检测框中离散选择索引的任务，利用单目视觉实现图像空间内的显式Grounding。Designs a 'name-then-track' mechanism, transforming target recognition into a task of discrete index selection from detection boxes, utilizing monocular vision to achieve explicit Grounding within image space.
推进: Advance: 证明了通过简化推理空间（从抽象CoT回归图像空间）可显著降低对多摄像头硬件的依赖，以算法巧劲弥补了低成本单目系统的视野局限。Demonstrates that simplifying the inference space (from abstract CoT regression to image space) can significantly reduce dependence on multi-camera hardware, using algorithmic ingenuity to compensate for the field-of-view limitations of low-cost monocular systems.

Data Pyramid for Embodied Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 具身操作领域面临真实数据稀缺、多源异构数据难以对齐以及仿真与真实世界域差异巨大的挑战，亟需一套系统化的数据分类与整合框架。The embodied manipulation domain faces challenges of scarce real data, difficulty in aligning multi-source heterogeneous data, and huge domain gaps between simulation and the real world, urgently requiring a systematic data classification and integration framework.
方法: Method: 提出“数据金字塔”架构，将真实机器人、UMI、人眼视角、仿真及通用数据分层，并明确各层级在可扩展性与机器人对齐中的角色与转换路径。Proposing a 'Data Pyramid' architecture, layering real robots, UMI, human-eye perspectives, simulation, and general data, and clarifying the roles and transformation paths of each level in scalability and robot alignment.
推进: Advance: 首次从理论层面厘清了低成本遥操作数据（如人眼视角）向高保真机器人数据迁移的层级关系，为利用仿真校准和域适应算法解决物理接触建模难题提供了明确的路线图。For the first time, clarifying the hierarchical relationship between low-cost teleoperation data (e.g., human-eye perspectives) and high-fidelity robot data from a theoretical perspective, providing a clear roadmap for utilizing simulation calibration and domain adaptation algorithms to solve physical contact modeling challenges.

RoboBRIDGE: A Modular Framework for Bridging Policies to Robust Real-World Robotic Agents
动机: Motivation: 针对当前VLA模型在真实物理环境中缺乏鲁棒性、遇到意外即失效的问题，旨在打破单纯依赖大模型参数扩展的局限，通过构建外部监控与规划框架来赋予其真正的Agent能力。Aiming to address the lack of robustness in current VLA models in real physical environments and their failure upon encountering unexpected events, this work seeks to break the limitations of relying solely on large model parameter expansion by constructing external monitoring and planning frameworks to endow them with true Agent capabilities.
方法: Method: 提出RoboBRIDGE模块化框架，将VLA模型封装并集成监控、规划与感知模块，利用LoRA微调将技能分解为MOVE、GRIP等域不变原语，同时引入外部LLM进行行为监控。Proposing the RoboBRIDGE modular framework, it encapsulates and integrates VLA models with monitoring, planning, and perception modules. It utilizes LoRA fine-tuning to decompose skills into domain-invariant primitives like MOVE and GRIP, while introducing external LLMs for behavior monitoring.
推进: Advance: 实现了不重新训练大模型即可快速适配新任务的低成本策略部署，有效解决了策略执行中的意外处理与任务分解难题，将VLA从‘做题学生’转化为具备环境交互能力的‘代理’。Achieving low-cost strategy deployment that quickly adapts to new tasks without retraining large models, this work effectively solves the challenges of handling unexpected events and task decomposition in strategy execution, transforming VLA from a 'student taking exams' into an 'agent' with environmental interaction capabilities.

AutoSpeed: Annotation-Free Stage-Adaptive Motion Speed Learning for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决现有模仿学习策略机械复现专家速度、无法根据任务难度自适应调整节奏的问题，以消除对昂贵速度标注的依赖。We address the issue where existing imitation learning strategies mechanically replicate expert speeds without adapting rhythms to task difficulty, thereby eliminating dependence on expensive speed annotations.
方法: Method: 提出无需标注的自适应速度学习框架，利用离散余弦变换（DCT）在频域隐式推断任务难易阶段并动态调整运动速度。We propose an annotation-free adaptive speed learning framework that utilizes Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) to implicitly infer task difficulty stages in the frequency domain and dynamically adjust motion speeds.
推进: Advance: 在不增加数据采集成本的前提下，实现了机器人运动节奏的自动化优化，显著提升了执行效率且保持了任务成功率。We achieved automated optimization of robot motion rhythms without increasing data collection costs, significantly improving execution efficiency while maintaining task success rates.

动机: Motivation: 试图解决人形机器人在非结构化工地环境中执行任务时，因形态差异导致的动作映射困难及Sim-to-Real对齐难题。We attempt to solve the difficulties in action mapping and Sim-to-Real alignment caused by morphological differences when humanoid robots execute tasks in unstructured construction site environments.
方法: Method: 构建感知与行动系统（VPA），通过PoseNet隐空间对齐与ActionNet师生蒸馏，将工人动作迁移至机器人并辅以RL控制。We construct a Vision-Perception-Action (VPA) system that aligns latent spaces via PoseNet and employs teacher-student distillation via ActionNet to transfer worker actions to robots, supplemented by RL control.
推进: Advance: 验证了基于隐空间对齐的遥操作方案在复杂物理接触场景下的可行性，为低成本构建人形机器人长视界策略提供了早期工程范式。We validate the feasibility of latent-space-alignment-based teleoperation schemes in complex physical contact scenarios, providing an early engineering paradigm for cost-effective construction of long-horizon strategies for humanoid robots.

Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有端到端策略在长程任务中缺乏规划能力且难以适应复杂物理交互的问题，旨在通过解耦规划与执行来降低对大规模高成本遥操作数据的依赖。Addressing the lack of planning capabilities in existing end-to-end policies for long-horizon tasks and the difficulty in adapting to complex physical interactions, aiming to reduce dependence on large-scale, high-cost teleoperation data by decoupling planning and execution.
方法: Method: 提出HiRoC框架，将视觉语言动作（VLA）模型进行分层后训练，利用大模型作为冻结的规划器生成高层指令，引导执行器完成具体动作。Proposing the HiRoC framework, which performs hierarchical post-training on Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, using large models as frozen planners to generate high-level instructions that guide executors to complete specific actions.
推进: Advance: 在LIBERO基准上将长程任务成功率提升至98%，证明了分层架构比扁平策略更能有效处理复杂任务，但同时也揭示了规划器冻结带来的鲁棒性风险及推理延迟增加的代价。Increasing the success rate of long-horizon tasks to 98% on the LIBERO benchmark, proving that hierarchical architectures are more effective than flat policies in handling complex tasks, while also revealing the robustness risks brought by frozen planners and the cost of increased inference latency.

推进: Advance: 验证了“视频预训练”在手术领域的可行性，证明了利用无标签视频进行数据高效学习的工程路径，但尚未在真机上实现完全闭环。Validating the feasibility of 'video pre-training' in the surgical domain, proving an engineering path for data-efficient learning using unlabeled videos, though a complete closed-loop has not yet been achieved on real machines.

方法: Method: 提出 EgoPHI 模型，利用仿真数据监督训练，从单目 Egocentric 视频中联合估计手部与物体的 3D 接触及接触力。Proposes the EgoPHI model, utilizing simulation data for supervised training to jointly estimate 3D contact and contact forces between hands and objects from monocular egocentric videos.

动机: Motivation: 针对ACT等冻结策略在微小扰动下易失效且传统重训练（如DAgger）成本高昂的问题，旨在探索一种无需重新训练主干网络即可低成本修正动作的机制。Aiming to explore a mechanism for low-cost action correction without retraining the backbone network, addressing the issue where frozen policies like ACT easily fail under minor perturbations and traditional retraining (e.g., DAgger) is costly.
方法: Method: 提出在线残差策略适应（ORPA）框架，在冻结的ACT主干之上叠加轻量级模块，利用人类反馈实时微调关节空间动作以动态修正执行偏差。Proposing the Online Residual Policy Adaptation (ORPA) framework, which overlays a lightweight module on top of the frozen ACT backbone, utilizing human feedback to real-time fine-tune joint-space actions and dynamically correct execution deviations.
推进: Advance: 相比传统重训练方法显著降低了数据收集与计算成本，实现了基于人类反馈的即时策略修正，但受限于人类反馈的低延迟获取难度及合成数据对复杂物理接触建模的不足。Significantly reducing data collection and computational costs compared to traditional retraining methods, achieving immediate policy correction based on human feedback, though limited by the difficulty of obtaining low-latency human feedback and the insufficiency of synthetic data in modeling complex physical contacts.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决接触丰富任务中物理触觉传感器昂贵且难以大规模部署的问题，试图通过算法预测替代硬件感知。Aiming to address the problem of expensive and difficult large-scale deployment of physical tactile sensors in contact-rich tasks, attempting to replace hardware perception with algorithmic prediction.
方法: Method: 提出PredTac框架，利用视觉输入在仿真与真机上预测接触点的触觉分布，以此作为策略学习的输入信号。Proposing the PredTac framework, which utilizes visual inputs to predict the tactile distribution at contact points in both simulation and real-world settings, using this as an input signal for policy learning.
推进: Advance: 将USB插入成功率从纯视觉基线的3.3%大幅提升至70%，证明了预测触觉在空间结构敏感任务中能有效逼近实测性能。Significantly increasing the USB insertion success rate from 3.3% for a pure vision baseline to 70%, proving that predicted tactile feedback can effectively approximate measured performance in spatially structure-sensitive tasks.

DexTouch-WM: Learning Action-Conditioned Tactile World Models from Human Touch for Dexterous Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人触觉数据稀缺且采集成本高昂的问题，旨在利用低成本的人类触觉演示数据作为“平替”来训练世界模型。Addressing the scarcity and high acquisition cost of robot tactile data, aiming to use low-cost human tactile demonstration data as a 'substitute' to train world models.
方法: Method: 通过对齐人与机器人手套的布局并执行动作空间重定向，将人类触觉数据注入动作条件化的触觉世界模型中。By aligning the layout of human and robot gloves and performing action space remapping, human tactile data is injected into an action-conditioned tactile world model.
推进: Advance: 证明了引入人类数据能显著提升机器人对接触动态的域预测能力，但揭示了合成数据生成策略在指导现实操作时存在不稳定性。Proving that introducing human data can significantly enhance the robot's domain prediction capability for contact dynamics, but revealing instability in synthetic data generation strategies when guiding real-world operations.

动机: Motivation: 针对H2R领域缺乏统一评估标准、任务与数据量混乱导致难以横向对比的痛点，旨在建立标准化的度量衡以验证仿真预测真机表现的能力。Addressing the pain point of the lack of unified evaluation standards in the H2R field, where chaotic tasks and data volumes make horizontal comparison difficult, we aim to establish standardized metrics to verify the ability of simulations to predict real machine performance.
方法: Method: 构建包含真实人类视频与仿真机器人环境的基准测试，通过固定机位视频输入驱动仿真环境，统一评估不同方法在桌面刚性物体任务上的迁移效果。We construct a benchmark test containing real human videos and simulation robot environments, driving simulation environments with fixed-camera video input to uniformly evaluate the transfer effects of different methods on desktop rigid object tasks.
推进: Advance: 首次量化证明了仿真环境能高相关性（r=0.89）预测真实机器人表现，为减少真机调试成本提供了可复用的“替身演员”验证框架。We provide the first quantitative proof that simulation environments can predict real robot performance with high correlation (r=0.89), providing a reusable 'stunt double' verification framework to reduce real machine debugging costs.

ARSTAG: An Agentic Real2Sim2Real System for Task-Specific Robot Data Generation
动机: Motivation: 为彻底摆脱对昂贵遥操作硬件和人工演示数据的依赖，探索利用Agent将单张静态图像自动转化为高保真仿真训练数据的可能性。To completely break dependence on expensive teleoperation hardware and manual demonstration data, we explore the possibility of using Agents to automatically convert single static images into high-fidelity simulation training data.
方法: Method: 设计ARSTAG智能体系统，结合LLM与工具链（如SAM3、GraspGenX），从单张RGB图像生成仿真场景与抓取数据，并通过随机化策略缩小Sim-to-Real鸿沟。We design the ARSTAG agent system, combining LLMs and toolchains (e.g., SAM3, GraspGenX) to generate simulation scenes and grasping data from single RGB images, and narrowing the Sim-to-Real gap through randomization strategies.
推进: Advance: 实现了从单图到仿真数据的端到端自动化生成，在通用抓取任务中达到74.6%的真实机器人成功率，显著降低了少样本场景下的数据获取门槛。We achieve end-to-end automated generation from single images to simulation data, achieving a 74.6% real robot success rate in general grasping tasks, significantly lowering the data acquisition threshold in few-shot scenarios.

Dexterous Robot Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Contact-Anchored Retargeting and Residual Policy Learning
动机: Motivation: 解决人类灵巧手与机器人手在形态与接触力感知上的巨大鸿沟，特别是传统方法难以捕捉物理接触细节导致仿真策略无法迁移的问题。Addressing the huge gap between human dexterous hands and robotic hands in morphology and contact force perception, particularly the problem where traditional methods fail to capture physical contact details, leading to the inability to transfer simulation policies.
方法: Method: 提出接触锚定重定向（Contact-Anchored Retargeting）方法，利用残差策略修正物理接触与动态可行性，将人类演示映射为机器人可执行的接触结构而非单纯关节角度。We propose the Contact-Anchored Retargeting method, utilizing residual policies to correct physical contact and dynamic feasibility, mapping human demonstrations into contact structures executable by robots rather than simple joint angles.
推进: Advance: 将数据清洗与物理一致性修复转化为可复用的流水线，有效解决了接触力缺失难题，在无需大量任务特定RL训练的情况下实现了仿真到真机的务实迁移。We transform data cleaning and physical consistency repair into a reusable pipeline, effectively solving the contact force deficiency problem and achieving pragmatic Sim-to-Real transfer without requiring extensive task-specific RL training.

InterMimicGen: Scaling Humanoid Loco-Manipulation through Self-Evolving Motion Imitation
动机: Motivation: 针对人形机器人 locomotion 与操作任务中高质量演示数据匮乏且采集成本高昂的痛点，试图打破对昂贵动捕设备与专用硬件的依赖。Targeting the pain points of scarce high-quality demonstration data and high acquisition costs in humanoid robot locomotion and manipulation tasks, this work attempts to break dependence on expensive motion capture equipment and specialized hardware.

Juno: Taming Predictive Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对现有视觉 - 语言 - 动作模型（VLA）在低成本数据下难以精准捕捉局部运动细节及物理世界动态变化的问题，旨在通过改进预测潜变量来增强模型对失败轨迹的学习能力。Aiming to address the problem that existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models struggle to precisely capture local motion details and physical world dynamic changes under low-cost data, by improving predicted latent variables to enhance the model's ability to learn from failure trajectories.
方法: Method: 提出Juno框架，利用动态CLS损失解决Token对局部运动不敏感的问题，并在测试时训练（TTT）阶段利用失败轨迹更新动力学模型以校准世界动态。Proposes the Juno framework, utilizing dynamic CLS loss to solve the insensitivity of tokens to local motion, and updating the dynamic model during Test-Time Training (TTT) to calibrate world dynamics using failure trajectories.
推进: Advance: 证明了仅凭60个演示数据即可通过失败反馈有效优化物理接触建模，实现了从单一模仿到利用失败数据校准仿真环境的低成本数据闭环。Proves that physical contact modeling can be effectively optimized through failure feedback with only 60 demonstration data, achieving a low-cost data loop from single imitation to calibrating the simulation environment using failure data.

高效仿真引擎与渲染Efficient simulation engine and rendering

本方向聚焦于通过高效仿真引擎与神经渲染技术，解决具身智能训练中数据稀缺与物理真实性难以兼顾的矛盾。演进脉络从早期基于简化物理模型（如MLP触觉模拟）追求速度，转向GPU并行架构（如ManiSkill3）提升吞吐量，再到引入神经渲染（如DiffusionRenderer、Gaussian Splatting）以生成式先验替代传统路径追踪。最新趋势强调“物理一致性”与“生成效率”的解耦与融合，利用世界模型（World Models）和生成式数字孪生，在保持视觉逼真度的同时确保物理交互的可靠性，旨在构建低成本、高保真且可大规模并行的Sim-to-Real闭环。This direction focuses on resolving the contradiction between data scarcity and physical authenticity in embodied intelligence training through efficient simulation engines and neural rendering technologies. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early speed-oriented approaches based on simplified physical models (such as MLP tactile simulation) to GPU parallel architectures (such as ManiSkill3) to improve throughput, and further to the introduction of neural rendering (such as DiffusionRenderer, Gaussian Splatting) to replace traditional path tracing with generative priors. The latest trends emphasize the decoupling and fusion of "physical consistency" and "generation efficiency," leveraging world models and generative digital twins to ensure the reliability of physical interactions while maintaining visual fidelity, aiming to build a low-cost, high-fidelity, and massively parallel Sim-to-Real closed loop.

2024-04
FOTS: A Fast Optical Tactile Simulator for Sim2Real Learning of Tactile-Motor Robot Manipulation Skills
动机: Motivation: 针对现有触觉仿真器（如TACTO）计算昂贵且难以在CPU上实时运行的问题，旨在降低Sim2Real触觉任务的数据采集与调试成本。Aims to reduce the data collection and debugging costs for Sim2Real tactile tasks by addressing the issues of high computational cost and the difficulty of real-time operation on CPUs in existing tactile simulators (such as TACTO).
方法: Method: 提出FOTS，利用MLP结合平面阴影技术替代复杂的有限元分析（FEM），在CPU端实现快速触觉渲染。Proposes FOTS, which utilizes MLP combined with planar shadowing techniques to replace complex finite element analysis (FEM), achieving fast tactile rendering on the CPU.
推进: Advance: 在CPU上实现28.6 fps的实时运行，显著快于传统方法，并在peg-in-hole任务中验证了较高的Sim2Real迁移成功率。Achieves real-time operation at 28.6 fps on the CPU, significantly faster than traditional methods, and validates a high Sim2Real transfer success rate in peg-in-hole tasks.

动机: Motivation: 传统物理路径追踪计算耗时且难以处理复杂光照材质，试图利用生成式先验加速神经渲染管线。Traditional physical path tracing is computationally expensive and difficult to handle complex lighting and materials, attempting to utilize generative priors to accelerate the neural rendering pipeline.
方法: Method: 将视频扩散模型（VDM）嵌入渲染流程，通过“脑补”光照与材质实现神经正向与逆向渲染。Embeds video diffusion models (VDM) into the rendering process to achieve neural forward and backward rendering by "hallucinating" lighting and materials.
推进: Advance: 实现了从单视频到可编辑G-buffer的转换，虽为黑盒但显著提升了处理效率与对噪声的鲁棒性。Achieves conversion from a single video to an editable G-buffer; although it is a black box, it significantly improves processing efficiency and robustness to noise.

OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有仿真器往往只关注任务是否完成，忽略了物理交互中的破坏性后果，导致训练出的策略在真实世界可能因“暴力成功”而损坏物体。Existing simulators often focus solely on task completion, ignoring destructive consequences in physical interactions, leading to trained policies that may damage objects in the real world due to 'violent success'.
方法: Method: 构建OopsieVerse基准，集成DamageSim模块，通过力、热、流体等近似模型量化机械与物理伤害，并在OmniGibson和MuJoCo上统一健康信号。We construct the OopsieVerse benchmark, integrating the DamageSim module to quantify mechanical and physical damage through approximate models of force, heat, and fluid, while unifying health signals on OmniGibson and MuJoCo.
推进: Advance: 首次将“安全”与“成功”解耦，使仿真环境能区分破坏性成功与安全成功，为具身智能提供了基于物理损伤的通用安全奖励信号。We decouple 'safety' from 'success' for the first time, enabling simulation environments to distinguish between destructive success and safe success, thereby providing a universal safety reward signal based on physical damage for embodied intelligence.

DA-Nav: Direction-Aware City-Scale Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对传统导航依赖昂贵SLAM地图且难以在大规模城市环境中进行低成本Sim-to-Real迁移的问题，旨在利用生成式先验替代精确物理建模。Targeting the problem that traditional navigation relies on expensive SLAM maps and is difficult to perform low-cost Sim-to-Real transfer in large-scale urban environments, it aims to utilize generative priors to replace precise physical modeling.

世界模型安全与评估World Model Safety and Evaluation

本角度聚焦于世界模型在具身智能中作为安全评估核心时的风险与防御。随着生成式世界模型从单纯预测转向决策规划，其内部幻觉与对抗脆弱性成为物理安全的关键隐患。核心思路是将安全评估从黑盒测试转向白盒生命周期管理，系统拆解预测偏差如何转化为物理事故。演进路径从早期关注数据分布偏移，转向深入分析模型生成逻辑中的对抗攻击面，并建立定性到定量的评估框架，旨在解决‘预测准确但行为危险’的悖论，为VLA模型提供可信的安全边界。This perspective focuses on the risks and defenses when world models serve as the core for safety assessment in embodied intelligence. As generative world models evolve from simple prediction to decision planning, their internal hallucinations and adversarial vulnerabilities become critical physical safety hazards. The core idea is to shift safety assessment from black-box testing to white-box lifecycle management, systematically dissecting how prediction biases translate into physical accidents. The evolutionary path moves from early concerns about data distribution shifts to in-depth analysis of adversarial attack surfaces in model generation logic, establishing a qualitative-to-quantitative evaluation framework. This aims to resolve the paradox of 'accurate prediction but dangerous behavior,' providing trustworthy safety boundaries for VLA models.

Kuration SDK: Addressing the Virtual2Real Gap via Data Curation
动机: Motivation: 揭示现有世界模型评估指标（如FVD、LPIPS）的高分与物理世界行为（如穿墙、瞬移）严重脱节，指出数据分布未对齐是虚拟到现实鸿沟的核心隐患。Reveals that high scores in existing world model evaluation metrics (e.g., FVD, LPIPS) are severely disconnected from physical world behaviors (e.g., wall penetration, teleportation), identifying misaligned data distributions as the core hidden danger of the virtual-to-real gap.

强化学习信用分配与多模态对齐Reinforcement Learning Credit Assignment and Multi-modal Alignment

在Sim-to-Real与强化学习信用分配及多模态对齐的交叉领域，核心挑战在于如何从复杂的视觉-动作轨迹中精准归因，并解决仿真与真实世界的分布差异。传统方法依赖人工设计奖励或计算昂贵的Shapley值，难以处理高维多模态数据。演进脉络显示，研究正从单一数值奖励转向利用大语言/多模态模型（LMM）作为“裁判”，将信用分配重构为相对排序问题。通过引入多模态比较机制，新方法不仅降低了绝对贡献计算的复杂度，还利用LMM的泛化能力缓解Sim-to-Real的域偏移，使策略学习更依赖语义对齐而非像素级拟合，尽管面临推理延迟与幻觉风险，但为黑盒信用分配提供了可解释的新范式。In the intersection of Sim-to-Real, reinforcement learning credit assignment, and multi-modal alignment, the core challenge lies in accurately attributing causes from complex visual-action trajectories and addressing distribution differences between simulation and the real world. Traditional methods rely on manually designed rewards or computationally expensive Shapley values, making them difficult to handle high-dimensional multi-modal data. The evolutionary trajectory shows a shift from single numerical rewards to utilizing Large Language/Multi-modal Models (LMMs) as 'judges,' reconstructing credit assignment as a relative ranking problem. By introducing multi-modal comparison mechanisms, new methods not only reduce the complexity of calculating absolute contributions but also leverage the generalization capabilities of LMMs to mitigate Sim-to-Real domain shifts. This makes strategy learning more dependent on semantic alignment rather than pixel-level fitting. Despite facing inference latency and hallucination risks, this provides an interpretable new paradigm for black-box credit assignment.

动机: Motivation: 传统强化学习中计算绝对信用贡献（如Shapley值）成本过高且难以直接应用于高维多模态感知，导致仿真到现实的策略迁移困难。In traditional reinforcement learning, calculating absolute credit contributions (such as Shapley values) is too costly and difficult to apply directly to high-dimensional multi-modal perception, leading to difficulties in strategy transfer from simulation to reality.
方法: Method: 提出MARS-RA框架，利用大语言多模态模型（LMM）作为裁判，将信用分配转化为多模态比较下的排序聚合问题，并引入Shapley值作为理论约束。Proposing the MARS-RA framework, it utilizes Large Language Multi-modal Models (LMMs) as judges to transform credit assignment into a ranking aggregation problem under multi-modal comparison, introducing Shapley values as theoretical constraints.
推进: Advance: 相比QMIX等基线，该方法在仿真与真实机器人任务中实现了更优的策略收敛，证明了利用LMM进行相对排序比绝对打分更能有效对齐多模态特征并提升鲁棒性。Compared to baselines like QMIX, this method achieves superior strategy convergence in both simulation and real-world robot tasks, proving that relative ranking using LMMs is more effective than absolute scoring for aligning multi-modal features and enhancing robustness.

RL$^2$-VLA: Adaptive RL Latent Compositional Steering with Test-Time Scaling for Vision-Language-Action Models

Accelerating Visual Policy Learning with Sampling-Based Model Predictive Control
动机: Motivation: 视觉策略学习在Sim-to-Real迁移中面临高维多模态数据下信用分配计算昂贵且难以归因的难题，传统端到端方法难以处理复杂的物理交互与分布差异。Visual policy learning faces the challenge of computationally expensive and hard-to-attributable credit assignment under high-dimensional multimodal data in Sim-to-Real transfer, where traditional end-to-end methods struggle with complex physical interactions and distributional shifts.
方法: Method: 引入采样式模型预测控制（MPC）作为“裁判”生成高质量轨迹，利用FoPG算法直接学习深度策略，将信用分配问题转化为对MPC生成轨迹的相对排序与拟合，而非直接优化像素级奖励。We introduce sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) as a 'critic' to generate high-quality trajectories, utilizing the FoPG algorithm to directly learn deep policies, transforming the credit assignment problem into relative ranking and fitting of MPC-generated trajectories rather than direct optimization of pixel-level rewards.
推进: Advance: 该方法利用MPC的规划能力缓解域偏移，使策略学习从依赖绝对数值奖励转向依赖多模态轨迹的语义对齐，显著降低了训练复杂度并实现了零样本真机迁移。This method leverages MPC's planning capabilities to alleviate domain shift, shifting policy learning from reliance on absolute numerical rewards to semantic alignment of multimodal trajectories, significantly reducing training complexity and enabling zero-shot real-world deployment.

Visuomotor Robotic Pruning in Planar Orchards Using Hybrid Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 针对传统Sim-to-Real方法依赖昂贵3D重建且难以处理高维视觉-动作轨迹归因的问题，旨在通过光流替代深度图来降低仿真到真实的分布差异。Aiming to solve the problem that traditional Sim-to-Real methods rely on expensive 3D reconstruction and struggle with attributing high-dimensional visual-action trajectories, we propose using optical flow instead of depth maps to reduce the distribution gap between simulation and reality.
方法: Method: 提出Hybrid-PPO结合离线演示，利用光流特征构建视觉-动作轨迹，并引入相对排序机制替代绝对数值奖励来评估修剪动作的有效性。We propose Hybrid-PPO combined with offline demonstrations, utilizing optical flow features to construct visual-action trajectories and introducing a relative ranking mechanism instead of absolute numerical rewards to evaluate the effectiveness of pruned actions.
推进: Advance: 成功将信用分配从像素级拟合转向基于光流语义的相对评估，显著降低了仿真训练成本，但在复杂户外场景下的泛化性仍受限于光流特征的鲁棒性。We successfully shift credit assignment from pixel-level fitting to relative evaluation based on optical flow semantics, significantly reducing simulation training costs, though generalization in complex outdoor scenarios remains limited by the robustness of optical flow features.

D-JEPA: A Decision-Aligned Latent World Model
动机: Motivation: 解决现有世界模型（JEPA）中预测准确但决策失效的矛盾，即潜在空间距离近不代表执行结果好，需将信用分配与决策结果显式对齐。We address the contradiction in existing world models (JEPA) where predictions are accurate but decisions fail, noting that proximity in latent space does not guarantee good execution outcomes, necessitating explicit alignment between credit assignment and decision results.
方法: Method: 设计D-JEPA引入关系算子，将执行结果硬编码回潜在几何空间，并将信用分配重构为基于候选集排序的相对问题，而非绝对预测误差最小化。We design D-JEPA to introduce relational operators, hardcoding execution results back into the latent geometric space and reconstructing credit assignment as a relative problem based on candidate set ranking rather than minimizing absolute prediction error.
推进: Advance: 证明了通过ordinal接口统一不同几何模型能有效提升决策成功率，但揭示了为追求精度而牺牲推理延迟及依赖大量候选集是Sim-to-Real部署的瓶颈。We demonstrate that unifying different geometric models through an ordinal interface effectively improves decision success rates, yet reveal that sacrificing inference latency for accuracy and relying on large candidate sets constitute bottlenecks for Sim-to-Real deployment.

Smoothness as a Constraint for Stable Humanoid Locomotion
动机: Motivation: 批判传统RL中依赖人工调参的平滑奖励项难以精准归因身体抖动与任务性能之间的因果关系，导致Sim-to-Real迁移不稳定。We critique traditional RL's reliance on manually tuned smooth reward terms, which struggle to precisely attribute causality between body jitter and task performance, leading to unstable Sim-to-Real transfer.
方法: Method: 提出DeCap框架，将信用分配从隐式奖励重构为显式的物理硬约束，通过分离上下半身约束机制，强制策略在语义层面满足物理稳定性。We propose the DeCap framework, reconstructing credit assignment from implicit rewards to explicit physical hard constraints; by separating upper and lower body constraint mechanisms, it forces policies to satisfy physical stability at the semantic level.
推进: Advance: 证实了显式物理约束比隐式多模态对齐更能有效缓解域偏移，实现了在异构地形上的零样本迁移，但暴露了固定约束在异构硬件上的泛化局限。We confirm that explicit physical constraints are more effective than implicit multi-modal alignment in mitigating domain shift, achieving zero-shot transfer on heterogeneous terrain, yet exposing generalization limitations of fixed constraints on heterogeneous hardware.

Action-Slot: Structured Action-Centric Representation Learning for Multi-Agent Atomic Activity Understanding
动机: Motivation: 针对多智能体场景中动作与物体解耦困难，传统方法难以从复杂视觉轨迹中精准分配原子动作信用，导致多模态对齐失效。Addressing the difficulty in decoupling actions and objects in multi-agent scenarios, where traditional methods struggle to precisely allocate atomic action credit from complex visual trajectories, leading to multi-modal alignment failure.
方法: Method: 提出Action-Slot将Slot Attention机制从物体识别转向动作中心表示，利用注意力差分机制在弱监督下实现动作与视觉轨迹的语义对齐。We propose Action-Slot, shifting the Slot Attention mechanism from object recognition to action-centric representation, utilizing an attention differential mechanism to achieve semantic alignment between actions and visual trajectories under weak supervision.
推进: Advance: 成功将信用分配从单一物体视角扩展至并发动作视角，实现了识别与定位的统一，但依赖仿真数据生成的假设限制了其在真实复杂动态场景中的归因能力。We successfully extend credit assignment from a single-object perspective to a concurrent-action perspective, achieving unified recognition and localization, though the assumption of relying on simulated data generation limits its attribution capability in real-world complex dynamic scenarios.

Opt2VLA: Force-Aware Vision-Language-Action for Contact-Rich Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决现有VLA模型仅关注几何位姿而缺乏触觉力控反馈，导致在接触丰富任务中无法精准归因力学交互信用，Sim-to-Real存在严重域偏移。We address the issue that existing VLA models focus solely on geometric poses lacking tactile force control feedback, resulting in an inability to precisely attribute mechanical interaction credit in contact-rich tasks and severe domain shift in Sim-to-Real.
方法: Method: 构建Opt2VLA，将触觉力控作为多模态输入直接嵌入VLA，利用触觉优化（TO）生成数据将信用分配重构为力参考值的预测问题，而非单纯几何规划。We construct Opt2VLA, embedding tactile force control as multi-modal input directly into VLA, utilizing Tactile Optimization (TO) generated data to reconstruct credit assignment as a prediction problem for force reference values rather than purely geometric planning.
推进: Advance: 实现了从“视觉语义对齐”到“力学语义对齐”的范式转变，显著提升了接触任务的Sim-to-Real迁移能力，但力控精度仍受限于底层控制器与预测模型的耦合误差。We achieve a paradigm shift from visual semantic alignment to mechanical semantic alignment, significantly enhancing Sim-to-Real transfer capabilities for contact tasks, though force control accuracy remains limited by coupling errors between the underlying controller and prediction models.

Credit-Guided Policy Improvement for Test-time Adaptive Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有测试时适应方法盲目修改策略导致信用分配失效及策略漂移的问题，亟需一种能区分有效改进与无效扰动的机制。Addressing the issue where existing test-time adaptation methods blindly modify policies, leading to credit assignment failure and policy drift, necessitating a mechanism to distinguish effective improvements from ineffective perturbations.
方法: Method: 提出信用引导策略改进（CGPI），通过计算观测变化与参考决策的相对信用评分，仅对高信用动作进行参数更新并回滚低信用操作。Proposing Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI), which calculates relative credit scores based on observation changes and reference decisions, updating parameters only for high-credit actions and rolling back low-credit operations.
推进: Advance: 将信用分配从依赖外部奖励重构为基于轨迹变化的相对排序问题，实现了无需额外奖励信号的黑盒信用归因，有效抑制了仿真到真实场景中的策略漂移。Reconstructing credit assignment from reliance on external rewards to a relative ranking problem based on trajectory changes, achieving black-box credit attribution without additional reward signals and effectively mitigating policy drift from simulation to reality.

方法: Method: 构建V-JEPA Policy，利用冻结的视觉-动作预测性潜空间编码器作为底座，仅训练预测器与动作专家，通过预测未来状态隐式学习因果结构。Constructing V-JEPA Policy, utilizing a frozen visual-action predictive latent space encoder as a backbone, training only the predictor and action expert, and implicitly learning causal structures through future state prediction.
推进: Advance: 证明了无需显式生成视频即可通过预测性表征实现高维多模态对齐，显著降低了计算开销并缓解了域偏移，确立了预测性表征在信用分配中的核心地位。Demonstrating that high-dimensional multi-modal alignment can be achieved through predictive representations without explicit video generation, significantly reducing computational overhead and alleviating domain shift, thereby establishing the core role of predictive representations in credit assignment.

Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA模型在仿真中依赖虚假相关性（如背景颜色）导致Sim-to-Real泛化失败，且缺乏有效解耦视觉幻觉与任务语义的信用分配机制。Addressing the failure of Sim-to-Real generalization in VLA models due to reliance on spurious correlations (e.g., background colors) in simulation, and the lack of effective credit assignment mechanisms to decouple visual hallucinations from task semantics.
方法: Method: 提出DILL方法，通过预测去除背景噪音后的未来状态，强制模型学习去除了虚假相关性的因果表征，从而分离视觉特征与动作信用。Proposing the DILL method, which forces the model to learn causal representations free of spurious correlations by predicting future states after removing background noise, thereby separating visual features from action credit.
推进: Advance: 通过预测性解耦机制将信用分配从像素级拟合转向语义因果推断，显著提升了模型在分布外环境下的鲁棒性，为多模态对齐提供了可解释的防幻觉范式。Shifting credit assignment from pixel-level fitting to semantic causal inference through a predictive decoupling mechanism, significantly enhancing model robustness in out-of-distribution environments and providing an interpretable anti-hallucination paradigm for multi-modal alignment.

HuMBLE: Human Motion-Driven Behavior Learning for Embodied Locomotion
动机: Motivation: 传统强化学习在Sim-to-Real迁移中难以从复杂视觉轨迹中精准归因人类运动意图，且纯数值奖励无法有效对齐高维多模态语义。Traditional reinforcement learning struggles to precisely attribute human motion intent from complex visual trajectories in Sim-to-Real transfer, and pure numerical rewards fail to effectively align high-dimensional multimodal semantics.

Energy-Efficient Gait Adaptation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Diverse Terrains
动机: Motivation: 单一奖励函数难以在Sim-to-Real中平衡能耗优化与动态稳定性，导致信用分配在复杂地形下失效。Single reward functions struggle to balance energy optimization and dynamic stability in Sim-to-Real, leading to credit assignment failure in complex terrains.
方法: Method: 构建分层强化学习架构，利用思维链（CoT）引导高层策略进行多模态能耗归因，底层策略专注于多模态状态下的鲁棒控制。Constructs a hierarchical reinforcement learning architecture that utilizes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to guide high-level strategies for multimodal energy attribution, while low-level strategies focus on robust control under multimodal states.
推进: Advance: 实现了高层语义目标与底层物理动作的解耦信用分配，通过多模态约束在真实世界实现了零样本部署与自适应能耗优化。Achieves decoupled credit assignment between high-level semantic goals and low-level physical actions, realizing zero-shot deployment and adaptive energy optimization in the real world through multimodal constraints.
2026-10
Many Ways to Succeed: Diversity-Driven RL Fine-Tuning for VLA Generalization
动机: Motivation: 现有VLA微调方法在Sim-to-Real中易陷入局部最优，缺乏对多模态成功轨迹多样性的显式信用分配机制。Existing VLA fine-tuning methods in Sim-to-Real are prone to getting stuck in local optima, lacking an explicit credit assignment mechanism for the diversity of multimodal successful trajectories.
方法: Method: 提出多样性驱动的RL微调（DRIVE），利用多模态比较机制显式奖励成功轨迹的多样性，打破策略收敛的单一性。Proposes Diversity-driven RL Fine-tuning (DRIVE), utilizing a multimodal comparison mechanism to explicitly reward the diversity of successful trajectories, breaking the singularity of strategy convergence.
推进: Advance: 揭示了RFT中全局收缩与模式发散的矛盾，通过多模态多样性约束有效缓解了Sim-to-Real中的分布差异，提升了OOD泛化能力。Reveals the contradiction between global contraction and mode divergence in RFT, effectively mitigating distribution differences in Sim-to-Real through multimodal diversity constraints and improving OOD generalization capability.

世界模型与物理因果推断World Models and Physical Causal Inference

本方向聚焦于利用世界模型（World Models）与物理因果推断解决Sim-to-Real迁移中的核心痛点：仿真与现实的分布差异及物理规律缺失。演进脉络从单纯的视频生成（如WorldLine）转向因果干预与潜空间预测（如Do-JEPA, V-JEPA），旨在剥离视觉表象与物理因果。近期工作进一步引入“自我进化”机制（EVO-WAM, RoboHarn-Evo）及部署后学习（DEWO），通过利用失败样本、物理反馈或VLM验证器，使模型在无需外部大规模重训下持续修正内部物理认知。核心思路正从“被动预测未来”向“主动干预与因果推理”转变，力求在低资源下实现高泛化的具身智能。This direction focuses on utilizing World Models and physical causal inference to address core pain points in Sim-to-Real transfer: distribution differences between simulation and reality and the absence of physical laws. The evolution has shifted from simple video generation (e.g., WorldLine) to causal intervention and latent space prediction (e.g., Do-JEPA, V-JEPA), aiming to decouple visual appearance from physical causality. Recent work further introduces 'self-evolution' mechanisms (EVO-WAM, RoboHarn-Evo) and post-deployment learning (DEWO), enabling models to continuously correct internal physical cognition using failure samples, physical feedback, or VLM validators without requiring large-scale external retraining. The core paradigm is shifting from 'passive future prediction' to 'active intervention and causal reasoning', striving for highly generalizable embodied intelligence under low resource constraints.

方法: Method: 将动力学学习与控制学习解耦，先利用海量无动作视频学习物理规律，再基于因果蒸馏训练控制策略。Decoupling dynamics learning from control learning, first learning physical laws using massive action-free video data, then training control policies based on causal distillation.
推进: Advance: 提出数据驱动的仿真器架构，显著降低了对动作数据的依赖，并在跨机械臂任务中验证了泛化能力。Proposing a data-driven simulator architecture that significantly reduces reliance on action data and validates generalization capabilities in cross-robotic arm tasks.

Recova: Agent-Guided Failure Recovery for Autonomous Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为解决具身智能在真实场景中因物理交互失败导致任务中断的痛点，需建立从失败样本中自动提取因果恢复策略的机制。To address the pain point of task interruption in real-world scenarios for embodied intelligence due to physical interaction failures, a mechanism is needed to automatically extract causal recovery strategies from failure samples.
方法: Method: 构建基于Agent的数字孪生试错系统，将失败案例转化为可复用技能库，并通过DAgger循环将恢复策略迁移至真机。Constructing an Agent-based digital twin trial-and-error system that converts failure cases into reusable skill libraries and transfers recovery strategies to real robots via DAgger loops.
推进: Advance: 成功将真机干预率从87.5%降至0%，证明了通过显式建模失败因果路径可实现无需人类持续介入的自主恢复。Successfully reducing the real-robot intervention rate from 87.5% to 0%, proving that autonomous recovery without continuous human intervention can be achieved by explicitly modeling failure causal paths.
2026-10
eRLT: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Action-Relevant Token Routing
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA模型在Sim-to-Real微调中样本效率低且难以捕捉动作相关物理因果特征的问题，探索轻量级参数优化方案。Addressing the low sample efficiency of VLA models in Sim-to-Real fine-tuning and the difficulty in capturing action-related physical causal features, exploring lightweight parameter optimization schemes.
方法: Method: 设计动作相关Token路由机制，在冻结VLA内部特征中动态抓取与任务动作强相关的信息，替代传统的全量微调。Designing an action-related Token routing mechanism that dynamically grabs information strongly correlated with task actions from frozen VLA internal features, replacing traditional full fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 在精细操作任务中将成功率从50%提升至90%，验证了通过特征路由而非重训基座模型即可高效学习物理交互因果。Increasing success rates from 50% to 90% in fine manipulation tasks, verifying that physical interaction causality can be efficiently learned through feature routing rather than retraining the base model.
2026-10
Demonstration-Calibrated Port-Hamiltonian Retuning for Manipulation Policies
动机: Motivation: 针对现有Sim-to-Real迁移中策略参数难以直接复用且依赖大量试错重训的痛点，旨在利用物理能量守恒原理在无需新数据收集的情况下优化控制器。Targeting the pain point in existing Sim-to-Real transfer where strategy parameters are difficult to directly reuse and rely heavily on trial-and-error retraining, this work aims to optimize controllers using the principle of physical energy conservation without new data collection.
方法: Method: 提出PHRetune离线方法，通过校准演示数据中的能量水平，对基于Port-Hamiltonian的阻抗控制器参数进行重调，以匹配Diffusion或VLA策略的动作输出。Proposes the PHRetune offline method, calibrating energy levels in demonstration data to retune impedance controller parameters based on Port-Hamiltonian systems to match action outputs of Diffusion or VLA policies.
推进: Advance: 首次将物理能量约束显式引入策略参数微调，证明了仅凭演示数据即可在LIBERO等基准上显著提升真实世界性能，无需额外训练数据。For the first time, explicitly introduces physical energy constraints into strategy parameter fine-tuning, demonstrating that performance in real-world benchmarks like LIBERO can be significantly improved using only demonstration data without additional training data.
2026-10
Robotic Boomerang Throwing via Model-Based Release Design
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人受限于执行器动力学特性，难以通过直接模仿人类动作实现复杂投掷任务（如回旋镖）的痛点，旨在解决仿真中物理参数拟合与真实物理规律缺失的矛盾。Aiming to address the pain point that robots, constrained by actuator dynamics, struggle to achieve complex throwing tasks (such as boomerangs) through direct imitation of human actions, and to resolve the contradiction between physical parameter fitting in simulation and the lack of real physical laws.
方法: Method: 提出基于模型释放的设计范式，不模仿人类动作轨迹，而是针对机器人能力优化释放状态与物体参数，利用气动系数拟合进行动力学建模。Proposing a model-release design paradigm that does not imitate human motion trajectories, but instead optimizes release states and object parameters based on robot capabilities, utilizing aerodynamic coefficient fitting for dynamic modeling.
推进: Advance: 打破了机器人只能进行直线投掷的局限，证明了通过“量体裁衣”的物理建模而非单纯动作模仿，可在低资源下实现高难度的具身技能迁移。Breaking the limitation that robots can only perform linear throws, demonstrating that embodied skill transfer at high difficulty under low resource conditions can be achieved through 'tailored' physical modeling rather than mere motion imitation.
2026-10
Juno: Taming Predictive Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 为了解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）中预测潜变量对局部运动不敏感，且缺乏利用失败样本修正内部世界动态认知的问题。To address the issue in existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models where predicted latent variables are insensitive to local motions and lack the ability to utilize failure samples to correct internal world dynamic cognition.
方法: Method: 引入动态 CLS 损失机制以增强对局部运动的感知，并在测试时训练（TTT）阶段利用失败轨迹更新动力学模型，实现类似 JEPA 的潜变量驯服。Introducing a dynamic CLS loss mechanism to enhance perception of local motions, and utilizing failure trajectories to update the dynamic model during the Test-Time Training (TTT) phase, achieving latent variable taming similar to JEPA.
推进: Advance: 验证了利用失败数据而非仅靠成功演示来修正物理认知是可行的，为在少样本场景下提升 VLA 的泛化性与物理因果推断能力提供了工程化路径。Validating that correcting physical cognition using failure data, rather than relying solely on successful demonstrations, is feasible, providing an engineering path to improve the generalization and physical causal inference capabilities of VLA in few-shot scenarios.
